CREATE TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL ,
  TRAINCATEGORY VARCHAR2(36) ,
  TRAINSCOPE VARCHAR2(36) ,
  THEME VARCHAR2(540) ,
  LECTURER VARCHAR2(270) ,
  TRAINTIME VARCHAR2(270) ,
  REMARK VARCHAR2(540) ,
  STATUS VARCHAR2(36) ,
  PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(18) ,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) 
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.TRAINCATEGORY IS '培训类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.TRAINSCOPE IS '内部/外部(培训范围)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.THEME IS '培训主题';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.LECTURER IS '培训讲师';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.TRAINTIME IS '培训时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.REMARK IS '备注说明';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.STATUS IS '状态(未提交,审核中,已退回,待实施,已实施)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.PROCESSSTATUS IS '流程状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN IS '培训计划';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN PRIMARY KEY (ID);
  
CREATE TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL ,
  TRAINPLANID NUMBER ,
  USERID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  USERNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) 
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.TRAINPLANID IS '培训计划ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.USERID IS '培训计划培训参与人员编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.USERNAME IS '培训计划培训参与人员名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER IS '培训计划参与人员';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_CTPU_TRAINPLANID ON T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER (TRAINPLANID);

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID, AUDITABLE, ATTACHMENT)
VALUES ('T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN', '1', '1');

VALUES ('T_CORE_TRAIN_PLAN_USER', '0', '0');

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL ,
  TRAINPLANID NUMBER ,
  TRAINMETHOD VARCHAR2(36) ,
  LOCATION VARCHAR2(270) ,
  TRAINTIME VARCHAR2(270) ,
  TRAININGMATERIAL VARCHAR2(270) ,
  ASSESSMENTMETHOD VARCHAR2(36) ,
  EXAMID NUMBER ,
  EVALUATION VARCHAR2(540) ,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) ,
  ACTIVATEDFLAG VARCHAR2(1) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  ACTIVATEDTIME DATE 
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.TRAINPLANID IS '培训计划ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.TRAINMETHOD IS '培训方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.LOCATION IS '培训地点';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.TRAINTIME IS '培训时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.TRAININGMATERIAL IS '培训教材';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.ASSESSMENTMETHOD IS '考核方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.EXAMID IS '试卷ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.EVALUATION IS '考核效果评价';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.ACTIVATEDFLAG IS '启用标志';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.ACTIVATEDBYID IS '启用人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.ACTIVATEDBYNAME IS '启用人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN.ACTIVATEDTIME IS '启用时间';
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN IS '培训实施';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_TRAIN PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_CORE_TRAIN_EXAMID ON T_CORE_TRAIN (EXAMID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_CORE_TRAIN_TRAINPLANID ON T_CORE_TRAIN (TRAINPLANID);

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_USER (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL ,
  TRAINID NUMBER ,
  USERID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  USERNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) ,
  ACTIVATEDFLAG VARCHAR2(1) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  ACTIVATEDTIME DATE ,
  SCORE VARCHAR2(60) 
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.TRAINID IS '培训实施ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.USERID IS '培训参与人员编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.USERNAME IS '培训参与人员名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.ACTIVATEDFLAG IS '启用标志/签到标志';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.ACTIVATEDBYID IS '启用人ID/签到人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.ACTIVATEDBYNAME IS '启用人名称/签到人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.ACTIVATEDTIME IS '启用时间/签到时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TRAIN_USER.SCORE IS '考核成绩';
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_USER IS '培训实施参与人员';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_TRAIN_USER ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_TRAIN_USER PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_CORE_TRAIN_USER_TRAINID ON T_CORE_TRAIN_USER (TRAINID);

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL ,
  TMPLNAME VARCHAR2(270) ,
  SINGLECHOICEQTY NUMBER(11) ,
  SINGLECHOICESCORE NUMBER(11) ,
  MULTIPLECHOICEQTY NUMBER(11) ,
  MULTIPLECHOICESCORE NUMBER(11) ,
  JUDGEMENTQTY NUMBER(11) ,
  JUDGEMENTSCORE NUMBER(11) ,
  SHORTANSWERQTY NUMBER(11) ,
  SHORTANSWERSCORE NUMBER(11) ,
  TOTALSCORE NUMBER(11) ,
  CITATIONQTY NUMBER(11) ,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) ,
  ACTIVATEDFLAG VARCHAR2(1) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  ACTIVATEDTIME DATE 
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.TMPLNAME IS '试卷模板名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.SINGLECHOICEQTY IS '单选题个数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.SINGLECHOICESCORE IS '单选题分值(/个)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.MULTIPLECHOICEQTY IS '多选题个数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.MULTIPLECHOICESCORE IS '多选题分值(/个)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.JUDGEMENTQTY IS '判断题个数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.JUDGEMENTSCORE IS '判断题分值(/个)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.SHORTANSWERQTY IS '简答题个数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.SHORTANSWERSCORE IS '简答题分值(/个)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.TOTALSCORE IS '试卷总分';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.CITATIONQTY IS '引用次数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.ACTIVATEDFLAG IS '启用标志';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.ACTIVATEDBYID IS '启用人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.ACTIVATEDBYNAME IS '启用人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL.ACTIVATEDTIME IS '启用时间';
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL IS '试卷模板';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_EXAM_TMPL PRIMARY KEY (ID);

VALUES ('T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL', '0', '0');

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_QUESTION (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL ,
  QUESTIONCATEGORY VARCHAR2(36) ,
  QUESTIONTYPE VARCHAR2(36) ,
  QUESTIONCONTENT VARCHAR2(540) ,
  ANSWER VARCHAR2(1080),
  JUDGEMENTANSWER VARCHAR2(36) ,
  QUESTIONTYPEORDERNO NUMBER(11) ,
  LASTUPDATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  LASTUPDATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  LASTUPDATEDTIME DATE ,
  LASTSUSPENDEDFLAG VARCHAR2(1) ,
  LASTSUSPENDEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  LASTSUSPENDEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  LASTSUSPENDEDTIME DATE ,
  ACTIVATEDFLAG VARCHAR2(1) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  ACTIVATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) 
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.QUESTIONCATEGORY IS '题目类别';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.QUESTIONTYPE IS '题目类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.QUESTIONCONTENT IS '题目内容';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.ANSWER IS '答案';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.JUDGEMENTANSWER IS '判断题答案';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.QUESTIONTYPEORDERNO IS '题目类型排序号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.LASTUPDATEDBYID IS '维护人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.LASTUPDATEDBYNAME IS '维护人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.LASTUPDATEDTIME IS '维护时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.LASTSUSPENDEDFLAG IS '注销标志(逻辑删除标志)(0-正常,1-停用) 默认为0';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.LASTSUSPENDEDBYID IS '注销人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.LASTSUSPENDEDBYNAME IS '注销人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.LASTSUSPENDEDTIME IS '注销时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.ACTIVATEDFLAG IS '启用标志';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.ACTIVATEDBYID IS '启用人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.ACTIVATEDBYNAME IS '启用人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.ACTIVATEDTIME IS '启用时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_QUESTION IS '题目';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_QUESTION ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_QUESTION PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_QUESTION_OPT (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL ,
  QUESTIONID NUMBER ,
  OPTCODE VARCHAR2(30) ,
  OPTCONTENT VARCHAR2(540) ,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) 
);
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_QUESTION_OPT.ID IS '主键';

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_EXAM (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL ,
  EXAMNAME VARCHAR2(270) ,
  QUESTIONMETHOD VARCHAR2(36) ,
  EXAMTMPLID NUMBER ,
  EXAMBEGINTIME DATE ,
  EXAMENDTIME DATE ,
  EXAMDURATION NUMBER ,
  EXAMCATEGORY VARCHAR2(36) ,
  SINGLECHOICEQTY NUMBER ,
  MULTIPLECHOICEQTY NUMBER ,
  JUDGEMENTQTY NUMBER ,
  SHORTANSWERQTY NUMBER ,
  TMPLSINGLECHOICEQTY NUMBER , 
  TMPLMULTIPLECHOICEQTY NUMBER ,
  TMPLJUDGEMENTQTY  NUMBER ,
  TMPLSHORTANSWERQTY NUMBER ,
  LASTUPDATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  LASTUPDATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  LASTUPDATEDTIME DATE ,
  ACTIVATEDFLAG VARCHAR2(1) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  ACTIVATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  ACTIVATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) ,
  CREATEDTIME DATE ,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) ,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) 
);

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM.EXAMENDTIME IS '考试结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM.EXAMDURATION IS '考试时长';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM.EXAMCATEGORY IS '试卷类别';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM.SINGLECHOICEQTY IS '单选题个数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_EXAM.MULTIPLECHOICEQTY IS '多选题个数';

 UPDATE T_CORE_PAGE_VIEW_CONFIG 
    SET CONFIG = '{"readonly": false}' 
  WHERE ID = 'T_CORE_EXAM_PAPER$ACTIVE$0';

 UPDATE T_CORE_PAGE_VIEW_CONFIG 
    SET CONFIG = '{"readonly": true,"button": {"core-exam-paper-review-detail-btn-back": {"hidden": false}}}' 
  WHERE ID = 'T_CORE_EXAM_PAPER$ACTIVE$1';
  
 UPDATE T_CORE_PAGE_VIEW_CONFIG 
    SET CONFIG = '{"readonly": false}' 
  WHERE ID = 'T_CORE_EXAM$ACTIVE$0';

 UPDATE T_CORE_PAGE_VIEW_CONFIG 
    SET CONFIG = '{"readonly": true,"button": {"core-exam-detail-btn-back": {"hidden": false}}}' 
  WHERE ID = 'T_CORE_EXAM$ACTIVE$1';
COMMIT;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG( 
    ID VARCHAR2(18)  NOT NULL,
    PACKAGENAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    MENUID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    PARENTMENUID VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    MENUNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    MENUICON VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    AUTHOR VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    FUNC VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG IS '代码生成器配置';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.PACKAGENAME IS '包名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.MENUID IS '菜单ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.PARENTMENUID IS '父菜单ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.MENUNAME IS '菜单名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.MENUICON IS '菜单图标';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.AUTHOR IS '作者(邮箱@前姓名简称)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.FUNC IS '功能模块';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE( 
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    CONFIGID VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    TABLENAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    TABLEDESC VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    TYPE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    FOREIGNFIELD VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE IS '表信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.CONFIGID IS '配置ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.TABLENAME IS '表名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.TABLEDESC IS '表描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.TYPE IS '主从表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.FOREIGNFIELD IS '关联主表字段';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_CCG_CONFIG_TABLE_CONFIGID ON T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_TABLE (CONFIGID);

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD( 
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TABLEID NUMBER NULL,
    FIELD VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    FIELDNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    FIELDLENGTH VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    FIELDTYPE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    REQUIRE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    DATAREQUIRE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    ORDERNO NUMBER NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD IS '字段信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.TABLEID IS '表信息id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.FIELD IS '字段';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.FIELDNAME IS '字段名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.FIELDLENGTH IS '字段长度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.FIELDTYPE IS '字段类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.REQUIRE IS '字段是否可为空';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.DATAREQUIRE IS '数据是否可为空';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.ORDERNO IS '排序码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_CCG_CONFIG_FIELD_TABLEID ON T_CORE_CODE_GEN_CONFIG_FIELD (TABLEID);

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_SQL_GEN_TABLE( 
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TABLENAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_SQL_GEN_TABLE IS '生成sql表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_TABLE.ID IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_TABLE.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_TABLE.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_TABLE.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_TABLE.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_SQL_GEN_TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_SQL_GENERATE_TABLE PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD( 
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TABLEID NUMBER NULL,
    FIELD VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    TYPE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    FIELDNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    FIELDLENGTH VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    FIELDTYPE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD IS '生成sql字段';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.TABLEID IS '表ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.FIELD IS '字段';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.TYPE IS '类型(增加、删除)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.FIELDNAME IS '字段名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.FIELDLENGTH IS '字段长度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.FIELDTYPE IS '字段类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_SQL_GENERATE_FIELD PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_CSG_FIELD_TABLEID ON T_CORE_SQL_GEN_FIELD (TABLEID);

-- 建表语句
/*
  数据表 年度计划主表
  备注：年度计划
*/

create table T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(32)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  annualPlanNo         varchar2(32)          null  /* 计划编号 */,
  planCategory         varchar2(32)          null  /* 计划类型 从系统编码取数 */,
  planYear             char(4)               null  /* 计划年度 */,
  estimatedDate        date                  null  /* 计划完成时间 */,
  createdById          varchar2(32)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(64)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(32)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(256)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(512)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_ANNUAL_PLAN_YEAR_CA */
create  index IDX_ANNUAL_PLAN_YEAR_CA on T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN(planCategory ASC,planYear ASC);
/* 创建索引IDX_ANNUAL_PLAN_NO */
create  index IDX_ANNUAL_PLAN_NO on T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN(annualPlanNo ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN is '数据表 年度计划主表 备注：年度计划' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.annualPlanNo is '计划编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.planCategory is '计划类型 从系统编码取数' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.planYear is '计划年度 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.estimatedDate is '计划完成时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN.remark is '备注 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 年度计划主表 T_CORE_ANNUAL_PLAN 语句结束===========*/

 comment on column T_CORE_EMP_TRAIN_PLAN.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EMP_TRAIN_PLAN.annualPlanId is '年度计划主表ID ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EMP_TRAIN_PLAN.userId is '用户id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EMP_TRAIN_PLAN.userName is '用户姓名 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EMP_TRAIN_PLAN.orgId is '用户科室id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EMP_TRAIN_PLAN.orgName is '用户科室名称 ' ;

 comment on column T_CORE_EMP_TRAIN_PLAN.organizer is '主办单位 ' ;

 comment on column T_CORE_EMP_TRAIN_PLAN.remark is '备注 ' ;

  inspectUserName      varchar2(64)          null  /* 送检负责人姓名 */,

) ;

create table T_CORE_INTERNAL_AUDIT(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(32)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  annualPlanId         number                null  /* 年度计划id */,
  annualPlanNo         varchar2(32)          null  /* 年度计划编号 */,
  auditNo              varchar2(32)          null  /* 内审编号 */,
  auditPurpose         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 审核目的 */,
  auditContent         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 审核范围 */,
  auditRule            varchar2(4000)        null  /* 审核依据 */,
  auditSchedule        varchar2(4000)        null  /* 审核日程 */,
  auditMethod          varchar2(4000)        null  /* 审核方式 */,
  auditDate            date                  null  /* 审核时间 */,
  orgId                varchar2(32)          null  /* 组织部门编码 */,
  orgName              varchar2(256)         null  /* 组织部门名称 */,
  reportRange          varchar2(4000)        null  /* 报告发放范围 */,
  reportContent        varchar2(4000)        null  /* 报告内容 */,
  auditResult          varchar2(4000)        null  /* 评审结论 */,
  createdById          varchar2(32)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(64)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(32)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(256)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(512)         null  /* 备注 */,

-- 建表语句
/*
  数据表 风险识别登记
  备注：风险识别登记
*/

create table T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  riskNo               varchar2(36)          null  /* 风险编号 */,
  riskDesc             varchar2(4000)        null  /* 风险描述 */,
  groupReviewResult    varchar2(4000)        null  /* 小组评审结论 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          timestamp             null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  constraint PK_RISK_IDENTIFICATION_REG  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICATION_REG */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST is '数据表 风险识别登记 备注：风险识别登记' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.riskNo is '风险编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.riskDesc is '风险描述 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.groupReviewResult is '小组评审结论 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 风险识别登记 T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_REGIST 语句结束===========*/

/*
  数据表 风险因素
  备注：风险识别登记
*/

create table T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  riskFactor           varchar2(4000)        null  /* 风险因素 */,
  parentId             number                null  /* 父项id */,
  dataLevel            number                null  /* 层级 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          timestamp             null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_RISK_FACTOR  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_RISK_FACTOR */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR is '数据表 风险因素 备注：风险识别登记' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.riskFactor is '风险因素 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.parentId is '父项id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.dataLevel is '层级 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 风险因素 T_CORE_RISK_FACTOR 语句结束===========*/

/*
  数据表 风险识别因素
  备注：风险识别因素
*/

create table T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  riskRegistId         number                null  /* 风险识别登记id */,
  riskFactorId         number                null  /* 风险因素id */,
  riskDesc             varchar2(4000)        null  /* 风险描述 */,
  riskResolution       varchar2(4000)        null  /* 风险处置计划 */,
  riskRate             number                null  /* 风险指数 */,
  severity             number                null  /* 严重度 */,
  incidence            number                null  /* 发生率 */,
  refWorkflowType      varchar2(32)          null  /* 关联流程类型 */,
  refWorkflowId        varchar2(32)          null  /* 关联流程示例Id */,
  beAuditOrgId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 责任部门编号 */,
  beAuditOrgName       varchar2(270)         null  /* 责任部门名称 */,
  carryMonitorRecord   varchar2(4000)        null  /* 实施监控记录 */,
  carryVerify          varchar2(4000)        null  /* 实施验证 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          timestamp             null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR */
create  index IDX_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR on T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR(riskRegistId ASC,riskFactorId ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR is '数据表 风险识别因素 备注：风险识别因素' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.riskRegistId is '风险识别登记id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.riskFactorId is '风险因素id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.riskDesc is '风险描述 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.riskResolution is '风险处置计划 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.riskRate is '风险指数 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.severity is '严重度 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.incidence is '发生率 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.refWorkflowType is '关联流程类型 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.refWorkflowId is '关联流程示例Id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.beAuditOrgId is '责任部门编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.beAuditOrgName is '责任部门名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.carryMonitorRecord is '实施监控记录 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.carryVerify is '实施验证 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 风险识别因素 T_CORE_RISK_IDENTIFICA_FACTOR 语句结束===========*/
commit;

/*
  数据表 调查信息
  备注：调查信息
*/

create table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  customerName         varchar2(600)         null  /* 客户名称 */,
  folderNo             varchar2(36)          null  /* 检品编号 */,
  surveyDate           date                  null  /* 调查时间 */,
  customerAdvice       clob                  null  /* 客户意见或建议 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY is '数据表 调查信息 备注：调查信息' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.customerName is '客户名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.folderNo is '检品编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.surveyDate is '调查时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.customerAdvice is '客户意见或建议 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 调查信息 T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY 语句结束===========*/

/*
  数据表 调查信息-内容
  备注：调查信息-内容
*/

create table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  surveyId             number                null  /* 调查信息id */,
  surveyContent        varchar2(600)         null  /* 调查内容 */,
  evaluate             number                null  /* 评价 */,
  evaluateDate         date                  null  /* 评价时间 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM */
create  index IDX_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM on T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM(surveyId ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM is '数据表 调查信息-内容 备注：调查信息-内容' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM.surveyId is '调查信息id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM.surveyContent is '调查内容 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM.evaluate is '评价 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM.evaluateDate is '评价时间 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 调查信息-内容 T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_ITEM 语句结束===========*/

  qualityAdvice        clob                  null  /* 质量负责人意见 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  constraint PK_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM  primary key  (id) /* PK_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM is '数据表 调查信息统计 备注：调查信息统计' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.statisNo is '编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.customerName is '客户名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.startDate is '开始时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.endDate is '结束时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.technicalAdvice is '技术负责人意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.qualityAdvice is '质量负责人意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 调查信息统计 T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM 语句结束===========*/

/*
  数据表 调查信息统计结果
  备注：调查信息统计结果
*/

create table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  surveySumId          number                null  /* 调查信息统计id */,
  surveyContent        varchar2(600)         null  /* 调查内容 */,
  total                number                null  /* 统计总数 */,
  verySatisfiedCount   number                null  /* 非常满意 */,
  satisfiedCount       number                null  /* 满意 */,
  commonlyCount        number                null  /* 一般 */,
  needsImprovedCount   number                null  /* 有待提高 */,
  dissatisfiedCount    number                null  /* 不满意 */,
  constraint PK_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET  primary key  (id) /* PK_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET */
create  index IDX_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET on T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET(surveySumId ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET is '数据表 调查信息统计结果 备注：调查信息统计结果' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.surveySumId is '调查信息统计id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.surveyContent is '调查内容 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.total is '统计总数 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.verySatisfiedCount is '非常满意 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.satisfiedCount is '满意 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.commonlyCount is '一般 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.needsImprovedCount is '有待提高 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SURVEY_SUM_RET.dissatisfiedCount is '不满意 ' ;

/*
  数据表 申投诉主表
  备注：申投诉主表
*/

create table T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  applyComplaintNo     varchar2(36)          null  /* 申投诉编号 */,
  applyComplaintCorp   varchar2(270)         null  /* 申投诉单位 */,
  applyComplaintDate   date                  null  /* 申投诉时间 */,
  folderNo             varchar2(36)          null  /* 检品编号 */,
  matName              varchar2(270)         null  /* 检品名称 */,
  delegateName         varchar2(60)          null  /* 代表姓名 */,
  delegatePhone        varchar2(18)          null  /* 电话 */,
  batchNo              varchar2(36)          null  /* 批号 */,
  producCorp           varchar2(270)         null  /* 生产单位 */,
  sampledCorp          varchar2(270)         null  /* 被抽样单位 */,
  reportPrintDate      date                  null  /* 报告打印时间 */,
  applyComplaintType   varchar2(60)          null  /* 申投诉类型 */,
  investigationResult  clob                  null  /* 调查结果 */,
  suggestion           clob                  null  /* 处理意见 */,
  qmResult             clob                  null  /* 质量负责人处理结果 */,
  feedback             clob                  null  /* 结果反馈 */,
  applyComplaintContent clob                  null  /* 申投诉内容 */,
  investigationUser    varchar2(60)          null  /* 调查负责人 */,
  investigationDate    date                  null  /* 调查时间 */,
  beAuditOrgNo         varchar2(60)          null  /* 责任部门编码 */,
  beAuditOrgName       varchar2(270)         null  /* 责任部门名称 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_APPLY_COMPLAINT  primary key  (id) /* PK_APPLY_COMPLAINT */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT is '数据表 申投诉主表 备注：申投诉主表' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.applyComplaintNo is '申投诉编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.applyComplaintCorp is '申投诉单位 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.applyComplaintDate is '申投诉时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.folderNo is '检品编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.matName is '检品名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.delegateName is '代表姓名 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.delegatePhone is '电话 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.batchNo is '批号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.producCorp is '生产单位 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.sampledCorp is '被抽样单位 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.reportPrintDate is '报告打印时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.applyComplaintType is '申投诉类型 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.investigationResult is '调查结果 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.suggestion is '处理意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.qmResult is '质量负责人处理结果 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.feedback is '结果反馈 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.applyComplaintContent is '申投诉内容 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.investigationUser is '调查负责人 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.investigationDate is '调查时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.beAuditOrgNo is '责任部门编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.beAuditOrgName is '责任部门名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.remark is '备注 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 申投诉主表 T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT 语句结束===========*/

CALL INSERTI18N('T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.SERVICE_NAME', '申投诉', '申投诉', 'zh-CN', '申投诉');
CALL INSERTI18N('T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.ID', '申投诉的主键', '主键', 'zh-CN', '主键');
CALL INSERTI18N('T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.PROCESSSTATUS', '申投诉的流程状态', '流程状态', 'zh-CN', '流程状态');
CALL INSERTI18N('T_CORE_APPLY_COMPLAINT.APPLYCOMPLAINTNO', '申投诉的申投诉编号', '申投诉编号', 'zh-CN', '申投诉编号');

-- 建表语句
/*
  数据表 新项目开展
  备注：新项目开展
*/

create table T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  projectNo            varchar2(36)          null  /* 编号 */,
  projectName          varchar2(600)         null  /* 项目名称 */,
  reason               clob                  null  /* 理由 */,
  resources            clob                  null  /* 所需条件及资源 */,
  carryOrgId           varchar2(36)          null  /* 实施科室编码 */,
  carryOrgName         varchar2(270)         null  /* 实施科室名称 */,
  groupReviewAdvice    clob                  null  /* 小组评审意见 */,
  groupReviewResult    clob                  null  /* 小组评审结论 */,
  qmAdvice             clob                  null  /* 质量负责人意见 */,
  carryTestContent     clob                  null  /* 实施检验内容 */,
  carryTestResult      clob                  null  /* 实施检验结论 */,
  reviewContent        clob                  null  /* 评审内容 */,
  reviewImprovedContent clob                  null  /* 评审改进内容 */,
  reviewTechnicalAdvice clob                  null  /* 评审技术负责人意见 */,
  reviewQmAdvice       clob                  null  /* 评审质量负责人意见 */,
  reviewRemark         clob                  null  /* 评审备注 */,
  confirmStandardName  varchar2(600)         null  /* 确认标准名称 */,
  confirmStandardNo    varchar2(270)         null  /* 确认标准编号 */,
  confirmContent       clob                  null  /* 确认内容 */,
  confirmResult        clob                  null  /* 确认结论 */,
  confirmQmAdvice      clob                  null  /* 确认质量负责人意见 */,
  confirmLeaderAdvice  clob                  null  /* 确认最高领导人意见 */,
  confirmRemark        clob                  null  /* 确认备注 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  constraint PK_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT  primary key  (id) /* PK_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT is '数据表 新项目开展 备注：新项目开展' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.projectNo is '编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.projectName is '项目名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.reason is '理由 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.resources is '所需条件及资源 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.carryOrgId is '实施科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.carryOrgName is '实施科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.groupReviewAdvice is '小组评审意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.groupReviewResult is '小组评审结论 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.qmAdvice is '质量负责人意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.carryTestContent is '实施检验内容 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.carryTestResult is '实施检验结论 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.reviewContent is '评审内容 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.reviewImprovedContent is '评审改进内容 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.reviewTechnicalAdvice is '评审技术负责人意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.reviewQmAdvice is '评审质量负责人意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.reviewRemark is '评审备注 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.confirmStandardName is '确认标准名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.confirmStandardNo is '确认标准编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.confirmContent is '确认内容 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.confirmResult is '确认结论 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.confirmQmAdvice is '确认质量负责人意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.confirmLeaderAdvice is '确认最高领导人意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.confirmRemark is '确认备注 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 新项目开展 T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT 语句结束===========*/

/*
  数据表 新项目开展实施
  备注：新项目开展实施
*/

create table T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  newProjectId         number                null  /* 新项目id */,
  folderNo             varchar2(36)          null  /* 检品编号 */,
  type                 varchar2(60)          null  /* 类别 */,
  sortor               number                default 1  null  /* 参数序号 */,
  testStandard         varchar2(600)         null  /* 检验依据 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM  primary key  (id) /* PK_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM */
create  index IDX_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM on T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM(newProjectId ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM is '数据表 新项目开展实施 备注：新项目开展实施' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM.newProjectId is '新项目id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM.folderNo is '检品编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM.type is '类别 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM.sortor is '参数序号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM.testStandard is '检验依据 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM.remark is '备注 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 新项目开展实施 T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM 语句结束===========*/

INSERT INTO T_CORE_SEQUENCE (ID, PATTERN, JSONPARAM) VALUES ('T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT', '[key][yyyy][nnn]', NULL);
INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID, AUDITABLE, ATTACHMENT) VALUES ('T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT', '1', '1');
INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID, AUDITABLE, ATTACHMENT) VALUES ('T_CORE_LAUNCH_NEW_PROJECT_ITEM', '0', '0');

/*
  数据表 保护客户机密调查表
  备注：保护客户机密调查表
*/

create table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  surveyNo             varchar2(36)          null  /* 编号 */,
  auditUserId          varchar2(36)          null  /* 监督员编码 */,
  auditUserName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 监督员姓名 */,
  auditDate            date                  null  /* 检查时间 */,
  beAuditOrgId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 责任科室编码 */,
  beAuditOrgName       varchar2(270)         null  /* 责任科室名称 */,
  beAuditOrgAdvice     clob                  null  /* 责任科室意见 */,
  qsOrgAdvice          clob                  null  /* 质量监督科意见 */,
  qmAdvice             clob                  null  /* 质量负责人意见 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY  primary key  (id) /* PK_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY is '数据表 保护客户机密调查表 备注：保护客户机密调查表' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.surveyNo is '编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.auditUserId is '监督员编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.auditUserName is '监督员姓名 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.auditDate is '检查时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.beAuditOrgId is '责任科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.beAuditOrgName is '责任科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.beAuditOrgAdvice is '责任科室意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.qsOrgAdvice is '质量监督科意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.qmAdvice is '质量负责人意见 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY.remark is '备注 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 保护客户机密调查表 T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY 语句结束===========*/

/*
  数据表 保护客户机密调查表内容
  备注：保护客户机密调查表内容
*/

create table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  surveyId             number                null  /* 调查信息id */,
  surveyContent        varchar2(600)         null  /* 调查内容 */,
  result               varchar2(600)         null  /* 结果 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 说明 */,
  constraint PK_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM  primary key  (id) /* PK_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM */
create  index IDX_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM on T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM(surveyId ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM is '数据表 保护客户机密调查表内容 备注：保护客户机密调查表内容' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM.surveyId is '调查信息id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM.surveyContent is '调查内容 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM.result is '结果 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM.remark is '说明 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 保护客户机密调查表内容 T_CORE_CUSTOM_SRT_SURVEY_ITEM 语句结束===========*/

-- 建表语句
/*
  数据表 外部评审
  备注：外部评审
*/

create table T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  auditNo              varchar2(36)          null  /* 外审编号 */,
  auditPurpose         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 外审目的 */,
  auditDate            date                  null  /* 外审时间 */,
  auditAddress         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 外审地点 */,
  auditUserName        varchar2(4000)        null  /* 参加外审人员 */,
  auditOrgName         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 检验机构名称 */,
  orgManager          varchar2(120)         null  /* 负责人 */,
  orgAddress           varchar2(270)         null  /* 地址 */,
  orgPostcode          varchar2(15)          null  /* 邮编 */,
  orgPhone             varchar2(60)          null  /* 电话 */,
  orgFax               varchar2(60)          null  /* 传真 */,
  rectifyResultDesc    varchar2(4000)        null  /* 整改完成情况 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT is '数据表 外部评审 备注：外部评审' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.auditNo is '外审编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.auditPurpose is '外审目的 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.auditDate is '外审时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.auditAddress is '外审地点 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.auditUserName is '参加外审人员 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.auditOrgName is '检验机构名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.orgManager is '负责人 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.orgAddress is '地址 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.orgPostcode is '邮编 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.orgPhone is '电话 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.orgFax is '传真 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.rectifyResultDesc is '整改完成情况 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT.remark is '备注 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 外部评审 T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT 语句结束===========*/

/*
  数据表 外部评审-审核组
  备注：外部评审-审核组
*/

create table T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  auditId              number                null  /* 外部评审id */,
  groupName            varchar2(36)          null  /* 审核组 */,
  groupUserId          varchar2(36)          null  /* 成员编码 */,
  groupUserName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 成员姓名 */,
  groupUserOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 工作单位 */,
  groupUserPhone       varchar2(60)          null  /* 联系方式 */,
  groupUserEmail       varchar2(90)          null  /* 邮箱 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP_auditId */
create  index IDX_CEAG_AUDITID on T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP(AUDITID ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP is '数据表 外部评审-审核组 备注：外部评审-审核组' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.auditId is '外部评审id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.groupName is '审核组 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.groupUserId is '成员编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.groupUserName is '成员姓名 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.groupUserOrgName is '工作单位 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.groupUserPhone is '联系方式 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.groupUserEmail is '邮箱 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP.remark is '备注 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 外部评审-审核组 T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_GROUP 语句结束===========*/


/*
  数据表 外部评审-不符合汇总

create table T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_NCR(

  beAuditOrgName       varchar2(270)         null  /* 责任部门名称 */,
  rectifyDeadline      date                  null  /* 整改期限 */,
  auditResultDesc      varchar2(4000)        null  /* 整改完成情况描述 */,
  ncrItemNos           varchar2(1200)        null  /* 不符合项编号 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_NCR  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_NCR */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_NCR_auditId */
create  index IDX_CEAN_AUDITID on T_CORE_EXTERNAL_AUDIT_NCR(AUDITID ASC);

/*
  数据表 不符合登记
  备注：不符合登记
*/

create table T_CORE_NCR(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  ncrNo                varchar2(36)          null  /* 不符合编号 */,
  ncrContent           varchar2(60)          null  /* 不符合内容 */,
  ncrStandard          varchar2(270)         null  /* 不符合依据 */,
  ncrGrade             varchar2(90)          null  /* 不符合严重程度 */,
  ncrRemark            varchar2(4000)        null  /* 不符合备注 */,
  beAuditOrgId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 责任部门编码 */,
  beAuditOrgName       varchar2(270)         null  /* 责任部门名称 */,
  beAuditUserId        varchar2(36)          null  /* 责任部门负责人编码 */,
  beAuditUserName      varchar2(60)          null  /* 责任部门负责人名称 */,
  ncrSource            varchar2(270)         null  /* 来源 */,
  ncrSourceNo          varchar2(90)          null  /* 来源编号 */,
  discoveryUserId      varchar2(36)          null  /* 发现人编码 */,
  discoveryUserName    varchar2(60)          null  /* 发现人名称 */,
  discoveryDate        date                  null  /* 发现时间 */,
  discoveryOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 提出部门编码 */,
  discoveryOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 提出部门名称 */,
  deadline             date                  null  /* 完成日期 */,
  checkStartDate       date                  null  /* 检查日期开始 */,
  checkEndDate         date                  null  /* 检查日期结束 */,
  checkType            varchar2(270)         null  /* 检查类型 */,
  measuresReq          varchar2(4000)        null  /* 措施要求 */,
  ncrConfirm           varchar2(4000)        null  /* 不符合确认 */,
  ncrReason            varchar2(4000)        null  /* 原因分析 */,
  rectifyPlan          varchar2(4000)        null  /* 纠正措施计划 */,
  rectifyCarry         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 纠正措施实施情况 */,
  verifyDesc           varchar2(4000)        null  /* 验证摘要 */,
  verifyResult         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 验证结论 */,
  nextVerifyResult     varchar2(4000)        null  /* 再次验证 */,
  nextVerifyDeadline   date                  null  /* 再次验证期限 */,
  verifyUserId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 验证人编码 */,
  verifyUserName       varchar2(60)          null  /* 验证人名称 */,
  verifyDate           date                  null  /* 验证日期 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_NCR  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_NCR */
) ;

/*
  数据表 质量控制
  备注：质量控制
*/

create table T_CORE_QUALITY_CONTROL(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  qcNo                 varchar2(36)          null  /* 质量控制编号 */,
  qcType               varchar2(60)          null  /* 质控类型 */,
  qcCorpName           varchar2(270)         null  /* 组织单位 */,
  carryDate            date                  null  /* 实施时间 */,
  qcContent            varchar2(4000)        null  /* 监控内容 */,
  qcBasis              varchar2(4000)        null  /* 评价依据 */,
  qcResult             varchar2(4000)        null  /* 结论 */,
  qcDeadline           date                  null  /* 完成期限 */,
  reviewUserId         varchar2(1200)        null  /* 评审人员编码 */,
  reviewUserName       varchar2(1200)        null  /* 评审人员名称 */,
  leaderUserId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 评审组长编码 */,
  leaderUserName       varchar2(60)          null  /* 评审组长名称 */,
  evnCondition         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 环境条件 */,
  resourceConfig       varchar2(4000)        null  /* 资源配置 */,
  qcPlanId			   varchar2(36)			 null  /* 质控计划ID */,
  qcPlanNo			   varchar2(36)			 null  /* 质控计划编码 */,
  planFeasibility	   varchar2(270)		 null  /* 计划可行性 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_QUALITY_CONTROL  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_QUALITY_CONTROL */
) ;

/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */

  testStandard         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 检验依据 */,
  equNo                varchar2(36)          null  /* 仪器编号 */,

) ;

/*
  数据表 纠正措施
  备注：纠正措施
*/

create table T_CORE_RECTIFY(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  rectifyNo            varchar2(36)          null  /* 纠正措施编号 */,
  discoveryOrgId       varchar2(60)          null  /* 发现部门编码 */,
  discoveryOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 发现部门名称 */,
  qualityUserId        varchar2(36)          null  /* 质量负责人编码 */,
  qualityUserName      varchar2(60)          null  /* 质量负责人名称 */,
  technicalUserId      varchar2(36)          null  /* 技术负责人编码 */,
  technicalUserName    varchar2(60)          null  /* 技术负责人名称 */,
  leaderUserId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 机构领导编码 */,
  leaderUserName       varchar2(60)          null  /* 机构领导名称 */,
  carryUserId          varchar2(36)          null  /* 实施审核人编码 */,
  carryUserName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 实施审核人名称 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_RECTIFY  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_RECTIFY */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_RECTIFY is '数据表 纠正措施 备注：纠正措施' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.rectifyNo is '纠正措施编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.discoveryOrgId is '发现部门编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.discoveryOrgName is '发现部门名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.qualityUserId is '质量负责人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.qualityUserName is '质量负责人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.technicalUserId is '技术负责人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY.technicalUserName is '技术负责人名称 ' ;

*/

create table T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  rectifyId            number                null  /* 纠正措施id */,
  orderNo              number                null  /* 序号 */,
  sourceType     	   varchar2(270)   		 null  /* 信息来源 */,
  processNo			   varchar2(36)			 null  /* 关联流程编号 */,
  discoveryDate        date                  null  /* 发现日期 */,
  beAuditOrgId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 责任部门编码 */,
  beAuditOrgName       varchar2(270)         null  /* 责任部门名称 */,
  rectifyGrade         varchar2(36)          null  /* 严重程度 */,
  deadline             date                  null  /* 解决时间 */,
  promDesc             varchar2(4000)        null  /* 问题描述 */,
  riskAssessment       varchar2(4000)        null  /* 风险评估 */,
  rectifyGuidance      varchar2(4000)        null  /* 指导措施 */,
  deviationReason      varchar2(4000)        null  /* 偏离原因 */,
  expectedDate         date                  null  /* 预计完成时间 */,
  causeanAlysis        varchar2(4000)        null  /* 原因分析 */,
  rectifyCarryPlan     varchar2(4000)        null  /* 纠正措施实施计划 */,
  rectifyResultDesc    varchar2(4000)        null  /* 完成情况 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  reviewResult         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 评审结论 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_RECTIFY_ITEM */
create  index IDX_RECTIFY_ITEM on T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM(rectifyId ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM is '数据表 纠正措施-子表 备注：纠正措施-子表' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.rectifyId is '纠正措施id ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.orderNo is '序号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.discoveryDate is '发现日期 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.beAuditOrgId is '责任部门编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.beAuditOrgName is '责任部门名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.rectifyGrade is '严重程度 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.deadline is '解决时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.promDesc is '问题描述 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.riskAssessment is '风险评估 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.rectifyGuidance is '指导措施 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.deviationReason is '偏离原因 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.expectedDate is '预计完成时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.causeanAlysis is '原因分析 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.rectifyCarryPlan is '纠正措施实施计划 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.rectifyResultDesc is '完成情况 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.createdByOrgName is '制单科室名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.remark is '备注 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.sourceType is '信息来源 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.processNo is '关联流程编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_RECTIFY_ITEM.reviewResult is '评审结论 ' ;

/*
  数据表 预防措施
  备注：预防措施
*/

create table T_CORE_PREVENTION(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  processStatus        varchar2(36)          null  /* 流程状态 */,
  preventionNo         varchar2(36)          null  /* 预防措施编号 */,
  discoveryOrgId       varchar2(60)          null  /* 发现部门编码 */,
  discoveryOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 发现部门名称 */,
  qualityUserId        varchar2(36)          null  /* 质量负责人编码 */,
  qualityUserName      varchar2(60)          null  /* 质量负责人名称 */,
  technicalUserId      varchar2(36)          null  /* 技术负责人编码 */,
  technicalUserName    varchar2(60)          null  /* 技术负责人名称 */,
  leaderUserId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 机构领导编码 */,
  leaderUserName       varchar2(60)          null  /* 机构领导名称 */,
  carryUserId          varchar2(36)          null  /* 实施审核人编码 */,
  carryUserName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 实施审核人名称 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_PREVENTION  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_PREVENTION */
) ;
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_PREVENTION is '数据表 预防措施 备注：预防措施' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.processStatus is '流程状态 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.preventionNo is '预防措施编号 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.discoveryOrgId is '发现部门编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.discoveryOrgName is '发现部门名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.qualityUserId is '质量负责人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.qualityUserName is '质量负责人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.technicalUserId is '技术负责人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.technicalUserName is '技术负责人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.leaderUserId is '机构领导编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.leaderUserName is '机构领导名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.carryUserId is '实施审核人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.carryUserName is '实施审核人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.createdById is '制单人编码 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.createdByName is '制单人名称 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.createdTime is '制单时间 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.createdByOrgId is '制单科室编码 ' ;

 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION.remark is '备注 ' ;
/*===== 创建表 预防措施 T_CORE_PREVENTION 语句结束===========*/

/*
  数据表 预防措施-子表
  备注：预防措施-子表
*/

create table T_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM(
  id                   number                null  /* 主键 */,
  preventionId         number                null  /* 预防措施id */,
  sourceType     	   varchar2(270)   		 null  /* 信息来源 */,
  processNo			   varchar2(36)			 null  /* 关联流程编号 */,
  orderNo              number                null  /* 序号 */,
  discoveryDate        date                  null  /* 发现日期 */,
  beAuditOrgId         varchar2(36)          null  /* 责任部门编码 */,
  beAuditOrgName       varchar2(270)         null  /* 责任部门名称 */,
  preventionGrade      varchar2(36)          null  /* 严重程度 */,
  reviewResult         varchar2(4000)        null  /* 评审结论 */,
  deadline             date                  null  /* 解决时间 */,
  promDesc             varchar2(4000)        null  /* 问题描述 */,
  riskAssessment       varchar2(4000)        null  /* 风险评估 */,
  preventionGuidance   varchar2(4000)        null  /* 指导措施 */,
  deviationReason      varchar2(4000)        null  /* 偏离原因 */,
  expectedDate         date                  null  /* 预计完成时间 */,
  causeanAlysis        varchar2(4000)        null  /* 原因分析 */,
  preventionCarryPlan  varchar2(4000)        null  /* 预防措施实施计划 */,
  preventionResultDesc varchar2(4000)        null  /* 完成情况 */,
  createdById          varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单人编码 */,
  createdByName        varchar2(60)          null  /* 制单人名称 */,
  createdTime          date                  null  /* 制单时间 */,
  createdByOrgId       varchar2(36)          null  /* 制单科室编码 */,
  createdByOrgName     varchar2(270)         null  /* 制单科室名称 */,
  remark               varchar2(600)         null  /* 备注 */,
  constraint PK_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM  primary key  (id) /* PK_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM */
) ;
/* 创建索引IDX_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM */
create  index IDX_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM on T_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM(preventionId ASC);
/* 把备注信息加到数据库系统属性表中 */
 comment on table T_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM is '数据表 预防措施-子表 备注：预防措施-子表' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM.id is '主键 ' ;
 comment on column T_CORE_PREVENTION_ITEM.preventionId is '预防措施id ' ;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_CORE_TEST( 
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TESTNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_TEST IS '测试';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TEST.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TEST.TESTNAME IS '测试名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TEST.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TEST.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TEST.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TEST.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_TEST.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_TEST ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_TEST PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_CORE_ITEM( 
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TESTID NUMBER NULL,
    ITEMNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    REPEATQTY NUMBER NULL,
    SCRIPT CLOB NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_ITEM IS '分析项';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.TESTID IS '测试编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.ITEMNAME IS '分析项名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.REPEATQTY IS '重复次数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.SCRIPT IS '计算公式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_ITEM.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_ITEM ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_ITEM PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_CORE_ITEM_TESTID ON T_CORE_ITEM (TESTID);

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_CORE_RESULT( 
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TESTID NUMBER NULL,
    TESTNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    ITEMID NUMBER NULL,
    ITEMNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    REPEATNO NUMBER NULL,
    ORIGINALRESULT VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_CORE_RESULT IS '结果';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.TESTID IS '测试编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.TESTNAME IS '测试名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.ITEMID IS '分析项编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.ITEMNAME IS '分析项名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.REPEATNO IS '重复数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.ORIGINALRESULT IS '原始结果';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_CORE_RESULT.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_CORE_RESULT ADD CONSTRAINT PK_CORE_RESULT PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CALL INSERTI18N('T_CORE_RESULT.SERVICE_NAME', '结果', '结果', 'zh-CN', '结果');
CALL INSERTI18N('T_CORE_RESULT.ID', '结果的主键', '主键', 'zh-CN', '主键');

CALL INSERTI18N('CORE.MODULE.TRAIN.EXAM_PAPER.TIP.COMPLETION_QUESTION_QTY_LESS_THAN_EXAM_COMPLETION_QUESTION_QTY', '题库已启用填空题数量不满足试卷填空题数量', '题库已启用填空题数量不满足试卷填空题数量', 'zh-CN', '题库已启用填空题数量不满足试卷填空题数量');

UPDATE T_CORE_EXAM_TMPL 
   SET COMPLETIONQTY = 0
 WHERE COMPLETIONQTY IS NULL;

   SET COMPLETIONSCORE = 0
 WHERE COMPLETIONSCORE IS NULL;

UPDATE T_CORE_EXAM 
   SET COMPLETIONQTY = 0
 WHERE COMPLETIONQTY IS NULL;
 
UPDATE T_CORE_EXAM 
   SET TMPLCOMPLETIONQTY = 0
 WHERE TMPLCOMPLETIONQTY IS NULL;
